provides a redundant but relevant punitive setting."},{"label":"weak","ref":"4:39","note":"Tail-audit spending from provision is a broad social contrast."},{"label":"no value","ref":"4:11","note":"Tail-audit inheritance rules have no relevant relation."},{"label":"no value","ref":"9:2","note":"Tail-audit travel period has no relevant relation."},{"label":"strong","ref":"7:50","note":"Denial of water and provision across the fire-garden boundary sharpens the focus's deprivation setting."},{"label":"medium","ref":"10:4","note":"Pairs punishment with شراب من حميم, a parallel punitive-intake profile."},{"label":"strong","ref":"14:16","note":"Explicitly assigns شراب من صديد in the postmortem punishment sequence, closely relevant to f02."},{"label":"strong","ref":"14:17","note":"Shows the compelled, failed intake of the preceding substance, directly reinforcing f01 and f02."},{"label":"strong","ref":"18:29","note":"The fire's drink is presented as injurious rather than sustaining, a direct functional parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"22:19","note":"حميم is poured over the condemned, directly joining punitive liquid with forced bodily entry."},{"label":"medium","ref":"22:20","note":"The bodily effects of the preceding حميم extend the focus's corrupted-intake consequence."},{"label":"strong","ref":"32:20","note":"Pairs punitive fire with ماء حميم, a concise parallel provision in the condemned setting."},{"label":"strong","ref":"37:62","note":"Contrasts competing نزل, directly opening the punitive hospitality sequence that includes forced eating and drinking."},{"label":"strong","ref":"37:63","note":"Names شجرة الزقوم as the central punitive provision in that sequence."},{"label":"medium","ref":"37:64","note":"Locates the punitive tree within the fire, strengthening the setting of its intake."},{"label":"medium","ref":"37:65","note":"Describes the tree's fruit, completing the food-form imagery before consumption."},{"label":"strong","ref":"37:66","note":"Explicitly makes the condemned eat from the punitive tree and fill their bellies."},{"label":"strong","ref":"37:67","note":"Adds حميم after that eating, directly pairing punitive food with punitive drink."},{"label":"medium","ref":"37:68","note":"Returns the consumers to the fire, completing the coercive punitive sequence."},{"label":"strong","ref":"38:57","note":"Pairs حميم with غساق, an especially close parallel to f02's washing-corruption boundary."},{"label":"strong","ref":"40:71","note":"Chains around necks establish coercion immediately before the hot-water movement."},{"label":"strong","ref":"40:72","note":"Moves the chained condemned through حميم and fire, a close forced-passage parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"44:45","note":"The food of the sinner is described as boiling within bodies, directly extending 44:44."},{"label":"medium","ref":"44:46","note":"Compares that boiling to حميم, linking punitive food and liquid."},{"label":"medium","ref":"44:47","note":"The command to seize the sinner reinforces the coercive administration of the punishment."},{"label":"strong","ref":"44:48","note":"Pouring the torment of حميم over the head directly supports forced punitive liquid."},{"label":"strong","ref":"47:15","note":"حميم cuts internal bodies, a direct anti-sustenance parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"55:43","note":"Identifies the fire setting immediately before circulation around boiling water."},{"label":"strong","ref":"55:44","note":"Links movement in the fire with حميم آن, a close punitive-liquid counterpart."},{"label":"strong","ref":"56:51","note":"Introduces the condemned consumers before the explicit زقوم intake."},{"label":"strong","ref":"56:52","note":"States eating from شجر من زقوم, a direct punitive-food parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"56:53","note":"Makes that eating fill the bellies, closely matching f01's failed food function."},{"label":"strong","ref":"56:54","note":"Adds drinking from حميم after punitive food, directly parallel to 37:67."},{"label":"medium","ref":"56:55","note":"Intensifies the compelled drinking image in the same sequence."},{"label":"strong","ref":"56:56","note":"Labels the preceding punitive meal as نزل, a direct hospitality inversion."},{"label":"strong","ref":"78:24","note":"Negates ordinary coolness and drink in the punishment setting, sharpening deprivation."},{"label":"strong","ref":"78:25","note":"Makes the exception حميم and غساق, an especially close f02 parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"88:4","note":"Places the food sequence in a scorching-fire setting."},{"label":"strong","ref":"88:5","note":"Pairs the punitive food with drinking from a boiling spring."},{"label":"strong","ref":"88:7","note":"Explicitly denies the food nourishment and relief from hunger, the clearest f01 parallel."},{"label":"strong","ref":"6:70","note":"Explicitly assigns شراب من حميم as punishment, a direct omitted punitive-intake parallel."}],"channel_subchannels_anchored_here":[{"parent_channel":"P2: Accounting becomes settlement of rights and wealth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Calculation issues in a binding settlement involving debt, obligation, property, authority, and the sufficiency or failure of accumulated means.","parent_surprising_reach":"The failed wealth scene reaches loan contracts, tribute, appraised reputation, marriage as sufficiency, land revenue, and a slave's staged payments toward release.","key":"B","name":"Capital, income, and the limit of sufficiency","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Property is acquired and produces income, yet is tested by whether it actually suffices or substitutes for the person.","active_motifs":"اتخاذ المال وكثرته (`quranic:root_001457:B001/m01`); الغنى والاستغناء (`quranic:root_001110:B001/m01`); الغَناء والكفاية (`quranic:root_001110:B002/m01`); الغنى بالمكان (`quranic:root_001110:B004/m01`); الكفاية والإغناء (`quranic:root_000318:B003/m01`); الحسب والمآثر (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`); دخل يخرج من أصل ثابت (`quranic:root_001102:B011/m01`); استهلاك المال وأخذه (`quranic:root_000043:B004/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`م و ل`: 69:28 (مَالِ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ)","synthesis":"Accumulated wealth, independence, adequacy, settled estates, inherited reputation, and property income define resources that appear durable. Consumption or seizure of others' property and provision as a source of livelihood test the moral quality of that wealth; the decisive question is whether any of it can stand in for the owner.","ayah_refs":["69:28","69:20","69:26","69:30","69:24","69:37","69:34","69:36"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.","parent_surprising_reach":"Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.","key":"A","name":"Sheltering orchard and dense growth","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Interlaced vegetation creates a covered garden whose density both conceals and sustains.","active_motifs":"الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); غشيان الليل (`quranic:root_000266:B002/m01`); البستان المستور بالشجر (`quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`); التفاف النبات واندفاعه (`quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`); المَجَنَّة موضع الاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B017/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ)","synthesis":"Cover, night-like enclosure, tree garden, dense growth, and a place of shelter make the orchard an inhabited canopy. Moist wooded ground, ripening fruit, and crop yield supply its productive interior, so concealment is life-supporting rather than evasive.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:24","69:34","69:36","69:37"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.","parent_surprising_reach":"Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.","key":"B","name":"Ripening, cutting, and gathered cluster","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Fruit reaches its time, is cut from its stem, and remains as an accessible cluster or usable remainder.","active_motifs":"أخذ الشيء بقطعه من أصله (`quranic:root_001241:B001/m01`); الثمر المقطوف والعنقود الداني (`quranic:root_001241:B002/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); ما يسقط من القِطاف (`quranic:root_001241:B004/m01`); نبات رخص عريض الورق (`quranic:root_001241:B008/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); إطعام الغصن وقبول الوصل (`quranic:root_000934:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); السلافة والخلاصة الأولى (`quranic:root_000733:B002/m01`); أكل القضى من عجم الزبيب (`quranic:root_001237:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)","synthesis":"Cut fruit, the cluster itself, harvest time, dropped gleanings, edible leafy plant, crop yield, ripened produce, accepted graft, emerging return, first pressed liquid, and eaten raisin pits trace a harvest from plant to gathered food and residue. Cutting here realizes maturity rather than destruction.","ayah_refs":["69:23","69:24","69:37","69:34","69:36","69:19","69:25","69:27"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.","parent_surprising_reach":"Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.","key":"D","name":"Past advance becomes present yield","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Earlier action is stored like seed, capital, or first provision and returns as consumable produce.","active_motifs":"التقدم والسبق (`quranic:root_000733:B001/m01`); السلفة قبل الطعام (`quranic:root_000733:B003/m01`); المال المقدم (`quranic:root_000733:B005/m01`); الأرض المسلوفة (`quranic:root_000733:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`); النصيب والرزق المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); مضي الزمن وذهاب القرن (`quranic:root_000436:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ)","synthesis":"What went before includes prior deed, advance meal, invested capital, and leveled seedbed. Its return appears as crop, allotted food, livelihood, and ripeness; elapsed generations give the causal sequence temporal depth. Reward is thus materialized as a yield whose conditions were laid down earlier.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:19","69:25","69:37","69:34","69:36"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hospitality joins appetite, vessel, and ease","parent_semantic_invariant":"A complete meal requires food and drink, allotted shares, serving vessels, bodily reception, and freedom from harmful aftermath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The table extends into pre-meal provision, a drinker's cohort, a sealed waterskin, congrats at good fortune, pasture for animals, and the social economy that sustains guests.","key":"A","name":"Meal, drink, portion, and shared company","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Food and liquid are taken in assigned shares by an eating and drinking company.","active_motifs":"تناول المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`); النصيب والرزق المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`); وعاء الأكل وموضعه (`quranic:root_000043:B013/m01`); تناول المائع (`quranic:root_000783:B001/m01`); نصيب الماء (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); جماعة الشرب والمشارب (`quranic:root_000783:B003/m01`); موضع الشرب وآلته (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`); ذوق الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); السلفة قبل الطعام (`quranic:root_000733:B003/m01`); هناءة بلا مشقة ولا وخامة (`quranic:root_001604:B001/m01`); الهنء عطاء وكفاية (`quranic:root_001604:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)","synthesis":"Eating, allotted livelihood, bowl, drinking, water-share, drinking group, room or vessel, taste, feeding, livelihood, first course, effortless digestion, and ample gift form the full hospitality frame. The meal is social because shares, servers, and companions are built into its lexical structure.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:37","69:34","69:36"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hospitality joins appetite, vessel, and ease","parent_semantic_invariant":"A complete meal requires food and drink, allotted shares, serving vessels, bodily reception, and freedom from harmful aftermath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The table extends into pre-meal provision, a drinker's cohort, a sealed waterskin, congrats at good fortune, pasture for animals, and the social economy that sustains guests.","key":"D","name":"Animal care at the edge of the feast","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Pasture, drink, coating, and fatness maintain the animals whose products support human provision.","active_motifs":"هناءة الماشية من النبت (`quranic:root_001604:B003/m01`); الهِناء قطران يطلى به البعير (`quranic:root_001604:B004/m01`); سمن الحيوان وطعم الشحم (`quranic:root_000934:B007/m01`); مستطعم الفرس وطلب جريه (`quranic:root_000934:B009/m01`); نصيب الماء (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); كرائم المال والإبل (`quranic:root_000001:B014/m01`); السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ)","synthesis":"Animals receive plant share, protective tar, body fat, induced running, allotted water, lush ground, and the status of choice stock; predation defines the risk at the boundary. Hospitality rests on an attended pastoral resource, not food appearing without material support.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:34","69:36","69:35","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Fire and washing are inverted into forced intake","parent_semantic_invariant":"Heat, fuel, hot liquid, bodily discharge, and food converge in a punitive process that reverses cooking, cleansing, and nourishment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The scene behaves like a furnace and bath gone wrong: fire consumes fuel, heat enters the body, washing residue becomes a meal, and an implement made to straighten by flame becomes an instrument of exposure.","key":"C","name":"Washing medium turned corrupt food","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Material associated with cleaning, runoff, or filtration is presented as the only available nourishment.","active_motifs":"تطهير الشيء بإسالة الماء وإزالة الدرن (`quranic:root_001087:B001/m01`); ماء الغسل وما يغسل به أو فيه (`quranic:root_001087:B002/m01`); ذوق الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); تناول المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`); خرقة تحبس صب الماء (`quranic:root_001102:B008/m01`); موضع الاغتسال وآلة التسخين (`quranic:root_000001:B013/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`غ س ل`: 69:36 (غِسْلِينٍ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)","synthesis":"Washing removes grime by flowing water and uses a washing medium or place; taste, feeding, eating, spout-filter, and bath vessel then redirect those sanitation materials toward ingestion. The reversal makes the absence of proper food concrete as contamination of the food category itself.","ayah_refs":["69:36","69:34","69:24","69:37","69:30","69:35"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Fire and washing are inverted into forced intake","parent_semantic_invariant":"Heat, fuel, hot liquid, bodily discharge, and food converge in a punitive process that reverses cooking, cleansing, and nourishment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The scene behaves like a furnace and bath gone wrong: fire consumes fuel, heat enters the body, washing residue becomes a meal, and an implement made to straighten by flame becomes an instrument of exposure.","key":"D","name":"Consumption, corrosion, and prey","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Eating ranges from nourishment to destructive consumption of body, property, and victim.","active_motifs":"استهلاك المال وأخذه (`quranic:root_000043:B004/m01`); إطعام النار واشتعالها (`quranic:root_000043:B005/m01`); التآكل والفساد والحكة (`quranic:root_000043:B006/m01`); الفريسة والمعدة للأكل (`quranic:root_000043:B007/m01`); الإفساد والنميمة بين الناس (`quranic:root_000043:B009/m01`); الإدعاء على الغير والتمكين منه (`quranic:root_000043:B010/m01`); قلة الجماعة بقدر رأس (`quranic:root_000043:B011/m01`); قوة الشيء وتمام مادته (`quranic:root_000043:B012/m01`); طعم العقل والقيمة (`quranic:root_000934:B008/m01`); القدرة على الشيء (`quranic:root_000934:B011/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`); الوقيعة والاغتياب كأذى السبع (`quranic:root_000669:B003/m01`); الخطيئة ذنب وإثم (`quranic:root_000420:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `خ ط ء`: 69:37 (خَٰطِـُٔونَ)","synthesis":"Property consumption, devouring fire, bodily corrosion, prepared prey, social sabotage, false accusation, a group small enough for one head, material strength, reason, power, throat-grip, predator, figurative mauling, and deliberate sin expose eating as appropriation. The punitive meal makes the eater resemble the corrupting appetite that defined the offense.","ayah_refs":["69:24","69:37","69:34","69:36","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Faith is tested as trust and provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Belief is not isolated assent; it creates security, worshipful orientation, and active responsibility for another person's food and dwelling.","parent_surprising_reach":"Faith's lexical image is a calm heart holding a trust, while neglect of the poor is rendered as refusal to set desire, household, land, and food in motion toward them.","key":"D","name":"Urging, reciprocal encouragement, and increased desire","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Speech and inward appetite are activated so that provision moves toward one who lacks it.","active_motifs":"الحَضّ على الشيء (`quranic:root_000334:B001/m01`); استزادة النفس (`quranic:root_000334:B004/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); الهنء عطاء وكفاية (`quranic:root_001604:B002/m01`); العناية الصادقة بالشيء (`quranic:root_001272:B015/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ض ض`: 69:34 (يَحُضُّ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ)","synthesis":"Exhortation can be mutual, and the self can be pressed to seek more on another's behalf. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
